Source references and excerpts
  • skill/scripts/search.py:18–39

    When the main guard is false, module evaluation completes without CLI acquisition or search.

     18  if __name__ == "__main__":
 19      if len(sys.argv) < 2:
 20          print("Usage: python google_search.py <query> [num_results]")
 21          sys.exit(1)
 22  
 23      query = sys.argv[1]
 24      num_results = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 5
 25      
 26      # We will pass these via env vars for security
 27      api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
 28      cse_id = os.getenv("GOOGLE_CSE_ID")
 29  
 30      if not api_key or not cse_id:
 31          print("Error: GOOGLE_API_KEY and GOOGLE_CSE_ID must be set in environment.")
 32          sys.exit(1)
 33  
 34      try:
 35          results = google_search(query, api_key, cse_id, num_results)
 36          print(json.dumps(results, indent=2))
 37      except Exception as e:
 38          print(f"Error: {str(e)}")
 39          sys.exit(1)

  • skill/SKILL.md:3–8
      3  description: Search the web using Google Custom Search Engine (PSE). Use this when you need live information, documentation, or to research topics and the built-in web_search is unavailable.
  4  ---
  5  
  6  # Google Search Skill
  7  
  8  This skill allows OpenClaw agents to perform web searches via Google's Custom Search API (PSE).
  • skill/SKILL.md:12–19

    Setup requires the API and credentials but supplies no complete HTTP interface specification.

     12  1.  **Google Cloud Console:** Create a project and enable the "Custom Search API".
 13  2.  **API Key:** Generate an API Key.
 14  3.  **Search Engine ID (CX):** Create a Programmable Search Engine at [cse.google.com](https://cse.google.com/cse/all), and get your CX ID.
 15  4.  **Environment:** Store your credentials in a `.env` file in your workspace:
 16      ```
 17      GOOGLE_API_KEY=your_key_here
 18      GOOGLE_CSE_ID=your_cx_id_here
 19      ```

Source references and excerpts
  • skill/SKILL.md:3–8
      3  description: Search the web using Google Custom Search Engine (PSE). Use this when you need live information, documentation, or to research topics and the built-in web_search is unavailable.
  4  ---
  5  
  6  # Google Search Skill
  7  
  8  This skill allows OpenClaw agents to perform web searches via Google's Custom Search API (PSE).
  • skill/SKILL.md:12–19

    Setup requires the API and credentials but supplies no complete HTTP interface specification.

     12  1.  **Google Cloud Console:** Create a project and enable the "Custom Search API".
 13  2.  **API Key:** Generate an API Key.
 14  3.  **Search Engine ID (CX):** Create a Programmable Search Engine at [cse.google.com](https://cse.google.com/cse/all), and get your CX ID.
 15  4.  **Environment:** Store your credentials in a `.env` file in your workspace:
 16      ```
 17      GOOGLE_API_KEY=your_key_here
 18      GOOGLE_CSE_ID=your_cx_id_here
 19      ```
  • skill/SKILL.md:27–27
     27  GOOGLE_API_KEY=xxx GOOGLE_CSE_ID=yyy python3 skills/google-search/scripts/search.py "OpenClaw documentation"
  • skill/scripts/search.py:3–14

    The function resolves requests from its defining module, independently of a CLI activation.

      3  import requests
  4  import os
  5  
  6  def google_search(query, api_key, cse_id, num_results=5):
  7      url = "https://www.googleapis.com/customsearch/v1"
  8      params = {
  9          'q': query,
 10          'key': api_key,
 11          'cx': cse_id,
 12          'num': num_results
 13      }
 14      response = requests.get(url, params=params)

Frozen source files

skill/SKILL.md
  1  ---
  2  name: google-search
  3  description: Search the web using Google Custom Search Engine (PSE). Use this when you need live information, documentation, or to research topics and the built-in web_search is unavailable.
  4  ---
  5  
  6  # Google Search Skill
  7  
  8  This skill allows OpenClaw agents to perform web searches via Google's Custom Search API (PSE).
  9  
 10  ## Setup
 11  
 12  1.  **Google Cloud Console:** Create a project and enable the "Custom Search API".
 13  2.  **API Key:** Generate an API Key.
 14  3.  **Search Engine ID (CX):** Create a Programmable Search Engine at [cse.google.com](https://cse.google.com/cse/all), and get your CX ID.
 15  4.  **Environment:** Store your credentials in a `.env` file in your workspace:
 16      ```
 17      GOOGLE_API_KEY=your_key_here
 18      GOOGLE_CSE_ID=your_cx_id_here
 19      ```
 20  
 21  ## Workflow
 22  ... (rest of file)
 23  
 24  ## Example Usage
 25  
 26  ```bash
 27  GOOGLE_API_KEY=xxx GOOGLE_CSE_ID=yyy python3 skills/google-search/scripts/search.py "OpenClaw documentation"
 28  ```
skill/scripts/search.py
  1  import sys
  2  import json
  3  import requests
  4  import os
  5  
  6  def google_search(query, api_key, cse_id, num_results=5):
  7      url = "https://www.googleapis.com/customsearch/v1"
  8      params = {
  9          'q': query,
 10          'key': api_key,
 11          'cx': cse_id,
 12          'num': num_results
 13      }
 14      response = requests.get(url, params=params)
 15      response.raise_for_status()
 16      return response.json()
 17  
 18  if __name__ == "__main__":
 19      if len(sys.argv) < 2:
 20          print("Usage: python google_search.py <query> [num_results]")
 21          sys.exit(1)
 22  
 23      query = sys.argv[1]
 24      num_results = int(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 5
 25      
 26      # We will pass these via env vars for security
 27      api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
 28      cse_id = os.getenv("GOOGLE_CSE_ID")
 29  
 30      if not api_key or not cse_id:
 31          print("Error: GOOGLE_API_KEY and GOOGLE_CSE_ID must be set in environment.")
 32          sys.exit(1)
 33  
 34      try:
 35          results = google_search(query, api_key, cse_id, num_results)
 36          print(json.dumps(results, indent=2))
 37      except Exception as e:
 38          print(f"Error: {str(e)}")
 39          sys.exit(1)
